This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ce-ModeA-r13 or if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB.
Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > MAC parameters > directMCG-SCellActivationResume-r16
Content: This field defines whether the UE supports having an E-UTRA MCG SCell configured in activated SCell state in the RRCConnectionResume message, as defined in TS 36.321 and TS 36.331 ; If the UE indicates support of directMCG-SCellActivationResume-r16, the UE shall also indicate support of resumeWithMCG-SCellConfig-r16.
Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > MAC parameters > directSCG-SCellActivationResume-r16
Content: This field defines whether the UE supports having an E-UTRA SCG SCell configured in activated SCell state in the RRCConnectionReconfiguration message contained in the RRCResume message, as defined in TS 36.321 , TS 36.331 and TS 38.331 . If the UE indicates support of directSCG-SCellActivationResume-r16, the UE shall also indicate support of ne-dc and resumeWithSCG-Config-r16 as specified in TS 38.331 . 4.3.19.25 directSCG-SCellActivationNEDC-r16 This field defines whether the UE supports having an E-UTRA SCG SCell configured in activated SCell state in the RRCConnectionReconfiguration message contained in the NR RRCReconfiguration message, as defined in TS 36.321 , TS 36.331 and TS 38.331 . If the UE indicates support of directSCG-SCellActivationNEDC-r16, the UE shall also indicate support of ne-dc as specified in TS 38.331 .

Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > Sidelink parameters > commSupportedBands-r12
Content: This field indicates the bands on which the UE supports sidelink communication, as defined in TS 23.303 and specified in TS 36.331 . If a UE supports sidelink communication on at least one band, the UE shall support sidelink communication transmission based on UE autonomous resource selection, eNB scheduled resource allocation, ProSe Per Packet Priority (PPPP) handling and out of coverage sidelink discovery. If a UE supports sidelink communication, the UE shall support 16 sidelink processes for reception of SL-SCH.
Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > Sidelink parameters > commSimultaneousTx-r12
Content: This parameter indicates whether the UE supports simultaneous transmission of EUTRA and sidelink communication (on different carriers) in all bands for which the UE indicated simultaneous sidelink and EUTRA support in a band combination (using commSupportedBandsPerBC).

Content: This field indicates the bands on which the UE supports sidelink discovery, as defined in TS 23.303 and specified in TS 36.331 .

It is mandatory for UEs of this release of the specification to support this feature if sidelink discovery is supported on at least one band (indicated by discSupportedBands-r12).

Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > Sidelink parameters > commMultipleTx-r13
Content: This parameter indicates whether the UE supports multiple transmissions of sidelink communication to different destinations in one SC period. If commMultipleTx-r13 is set to supported then the UE supports 8 transmitting sidelink processes.
Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > Sidelink parameters > discInterFreqTx-r13
Content: This parameter indicates whether the UE supports sidelink discovery announcements either a) on the primary frequency only or b) on other frequencies also, regardless of the UE configuration (e.g. CA, DC). The UE may set discInterFreqTx-r13 to supported when having a separate transmitter or if it can request sidelink discovery transmission gaps.
Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > Sidelink parameters > discPeriodicSLSS-r13
Content: This parameter indicates whether the UE supports periodic Sidelink Synchronization Signal (SLSS) transmission and reception for sidelink discovery. It is mandatory for UEs to support this feature if sidelink PS discovery is supported and it is optional otherwise.

Content: This parameter indicates whether the UE supports sidelink transmission/reception on the band in the band combination. For NR sidelink transmission, tx-Sidelink-r16 is only applicable if the UE supports at least one of sl-TransmissionMode1-r16 and sl-TransmissionMode2-r16 on the band as specified in TS 38.331 . For NR sidelink reception, rx-Sidelink-r16 is only applicable if the UE supports sl-Reception-r16 on the band as specified in TS 38.331 . 4.3.21.35 sl-A2X-Service-r18 This parameter indicates whether the UE supports A2X service and dedicated resource pool for A2X service as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > SC-PTM parameters > scptm-ParallelReception-r13
Content: This parameter defines whether UEs supporting SC-PTM support the parallel reception of DL-SCH transport block(s) associated with G-RNTI/SC-RNTI and DL-SCH transport block(s) associated with C-RNTI/Semi-Persistent Scheduling C-RNTI as well as the parallel reception of multiple DL-SCH transport blocks associated with G-RNTI/SC-RNTI in the same subframe. In SC-PTM operation, the DL-SCH processing capability is shared between the DL-SCH transport block(s) associated with G-RNTI/SC-RNTI and the DL-SCH transport block(s) associated with C-RNTI/Semi-Persistent Scheduling C-RNTI. A UE that supports scptm-ParallelReception-r13 shall also support SC-PTM reception in RRC_CONNECTED and in RRC_IDLE according to SC-PTM procedures as specified in TS 36.331 , TS 36.321 and TS 36.304 .

Content: This parameter defines whether UEs supporting SC-PTM support in RRC_CONNECTED, MBMS reception via SC-PTM on a frequency indicated in an MBMSInterestIndication message, where (according to supportedBandCombination and to network synchronization properties) a serving cell may be additionally configured, as specified in TS 36.331 . If this is supported, the UE shall also support MBMS reception via SC-PTM on a frequency when an SCell is configured on that frequency (regardless of whether the SCell is activated or deactivated), as specified in TS 36.331 .

Content: Only applicable if the UE supports LWA. This parameter defines whether the UE supports split bearer operation in LWA, i.e. the capability to receive data transmission for the same DRB on both LTE and WLAN simultaneously.

Content: Only applicable if the UE supports LWA. This field indicates whether the UE supports the layer 2 buffer sizes corresponding to "with support for split bearers" columns defined in Tables 4.1-3 and 4.1A-3.

Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > EBF FD-MIMO parameters > beamformed-r13
Content: Indicates the UE capabilities concerning beamformed EBF/ FD-MIMO operation (class B), see TS 36.213 , clause 7.2.5. The capabilities comprise of a list of pairs of \ k-Max, n-MaxList\ values with the nth entry indicating the values that the UE supports for each CSI process in case n CSI processes would be configured, with: k-Max: Indicating the maximum number of NZP CSI RS resource configurations supported n-Max: Indicating the maximum number of NZP CSI RS ports supported within a CSI process. The capability parameters are provided separately per transmission mode (TM9, TM10), which is applicable for all bands of band combinations except when additionally included per band of band combination per TM indicating the concerned capability is different from the per TM capability.

Content: Indicates whether the UE supports DMRS enhancements for the indicated transmission mode, see TS 36.213 , clause 7.1.5B and TS 36.212 , clauses 5.3.3.1.5C/ D. The capability parameter is provided separately per transmission mode (TM9, TM10), which is applicable for all bands of band combinations except when additionally included per band of band combination per TM indicating the concerned capability is different from the per TM capability. This field is absent when the FD-MIMO capability is provided as part of sTTI/sPT band combinations.

Content: Indicates the UE capabilities concerning non-precoded EBF/ FD-MIMO operation (class A) for CSI-RS and CSI reporting using 8, 12 and 16 antenna ports, see TS 36.213 , clause 7.2. config1: Indicates support of codebook configuration 1. config2: Indicates support of codebook configuration 2. config3: Indicates support of codebook configuration 3. config4: Indicates support of codebook configuration 4. The capability parameters are provided separately per transmission mode (TM9, TM10), which is applicable for all bands of band combinations except when additionally included per band of band combination per TM indicating the concerned capability is different from the per TM capability. See also TS 36.331 clause 6.3.6, NOTE 8 in UE-EUTRA-Capability field descriptions.

Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > EBF FD-MIMO parameters > csi-ReportingAdvanced-r14, csi-ReportingAdvancedMaxPorts-r14
Content: Indicates the maximum number of CSI-RS ports supported by the UE for advanced CSI reporting. The field csi-ReportingAdvanced-r14 is included to indicate 32 CSI-RS ports whereas csi-ReportingAdvancedMaxPorts-r14 is included to indicate 8, 12, 16, 20, 24 or 28 CSI-RS ports (i.e., UE shall not include both csi-ReportingAdvanced-r14 and csi-ReportingAdvancedMaxPorts-r14). The capability parameter is provided separately per transmission mode (TM9, TM10), which is applicable for all bands of band combinations except when additionally included per band of band combination per TM indicating the concerned capability is different from the per TM capability.

Content: Indicates whether the UE supports CSI reporting on non-precoded CSI-RS with 20, 24, 28 or 32 antenna ports, see TS 36.213 [22[, Table 7.2.4-9. The capability parameter is provided separately per transmission mode (TM9, TM10), which is applicable for all bands of band combinations except when additionally included per band of band combination per TM indicating the concerned capability is different from the per TM capability. See also TS 36.331 clause 6.3.6, NOTE 8 in UE-EUTRA-Capability field descriptions. A UE indicating support of csi-ReportingNP-r14 shall also indicate support of nonPrecoded-r13.

Content: This field indicates relative weight of processing FD-MIMO with 2/ 4/ 8 layers with respect to non-FD-MIMO with the same number of layers, as described in equation 4.3.28.13-1 and TS 36.331 clause 6.3.6, NOTE 8 in UE-EUTRA-Capability field descriptions. This field can be included only if the UE supports the corresponding number of layers (i.e. 2/ 4/ 8 layers).

Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > IoT NTN parameters > ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17
Content: This field indicates whether the UE supports NTN access. This field is only applicable if the UE supports ce-ModeA-r13 or any ue-Category-NB. If the UE indicates this capability the UE shall support the following enhancements: General: handling of cellBarred-NTN-r17 and trackingAreaList-r17 in SystemInformationBlockType1(-NB) as specified in TS 36.331 ; reception of SystemInformationBlockType31(-NB) as specified in TS 36.331 ; derivation of its position based on its GNSS measurements; reporting of the remaining GNSS validity duration as specified in TS 36.331 ; PDCP: if the UE supports ce-ModeA-r13, discardTimerExt-r17 as specified in TS 36.331 ; RLC: t-ReorderingExt-r17 as specified in TS 36.331 ; MAC: estimation of UE-gNB RTT as specified in TS 36.321 ; delaying the start of the RA response window as specified in TS 36.321 ; delaying the start of the mac-ContentionResolutionTimer as specified in TS 36.321 ; if the UE supports ce-ModeA-r13 or if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB and supports sr-WithoutHARQ-ACK-r15, handling of sr-ProhibitTimerOffset-r17 as specified in TS 36.331 ; extending the length of the (UL) HARQ RTT timer as specified in TS 36.321 ; Physical layer: calculation of the UE specific TA in RRC_IDLE and RRC_CONNECTED state based on its GNSS-acquired position and the serving satellite ephemeris as specified in TS 36.211 ; calculation of the common TA in RRC_IDLE and RRC_CONNECTED as specified in TS 36.213 ; for TA update in RRC_CONNECTED state, support of combination of both open (i.e. UE specific TA estimation, and common TA calculation) and closed (i.e., received TA commands) control loops; frequency pre-compensation to counter shift the Doppler experienced on the service link; timing relationship enhancements using higher layer parameters k-Offset-r17 and k-Mac-r17 as specified in TS 36.213 ; segmented UL transmission using higher layer parameters prach-TxDuration-r17, nprach-TxDurationFmt01-r17, nprach-TxDurationFmt2-r17, pucch-TxDuration-r17 and (n)pusch-TxDuration-r17 as specified in TS 36.331 except for UEs indicating support of ue-Category-NB and ntn-ScenarioSupport-r17 with value GSO. A UE indicating support of ce-ModeA-r13 and ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17 shall also indicate support of standaloneGNSS-Location. A UE indicating support for any ue-Category-NB and ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17 is assumed to have GNSS location capability.
Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > IoT NTN parameters > ntn-TA-Report-r17
Content: This field indicates whether the UE supports Timing advance reporting in NTN cell as specified in TS 36.321 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17.

Content: This field indicates whether the UE supports timing relationship enhancements using Differential Koffset as specified in TS 36.321 and TS 36.213 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17.
Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > IoT NTN parameters > ntn-ScenarioSupport-r17
Content: This field indicates whether the UE supports NTN features in GSO or NGSO scenario. The UE indicating support of ntn-ScenarioSupport-r17 shall also indicate support of ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17. If a UE does not include this field but includes ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17, the UE supports the NTN features for both GSO and NGSO scenarios.
Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > IoT NTN parameters > ntn-SegmentedPrecompensationGaps-r17
Content: This field indicates the supported gap length between segments for PUSCH and PUCCH required by a UE supporting ce-ModeA-r13 or for NPUSCH required by a UE supporting ue-category-NB, for TA pre-compensation. This feature is only applicable if the UE supports either ue-category-NB or ce-ModeA-r13 and also supports ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17. If a UE does not include this field but includes ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17, in case of overlapped transmission between successive uplink segments, UE shall follow the procedure specified in TS 36.213 . This field is not applicable for UEs indicating support of ue-Category-NB and ntn-ScenarioSupport-r17 with value GSO.

Content: This field indicates whether the UE supports location-based conditional handover for earth moving cell, i.e., CondEvent D1 as specified in TS 36.331 . A UE supporting this feature shall also indicate the support of cho-r16 and ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17.
Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > IoT NTN parameters > ntn-TimeBasedCHO-r18
Content: This field indicates whether the UE supports time-based conditional handover, i.e., CondEvent T1 as specified in TS 36.331 . A UE supporting this feature shall also indicate the support of cho-r16 and ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17.

Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > IoT NTN parameters > ntn-RRC-HarqDisableSingleTB-r18

This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ue-category-NB. A UE supporting this feature shall also indicate the support of ntn-RRC-HarqDisableSingleTB-r18.
Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > IoT NTN parameters > ntn-DCI-HarqDisableSingleTB-r18

This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ue-category-NB. A UE supporting this feature shall also indicate the support of ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17.

This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ue-category-NB. A UE supporting this feature shall also indicate the support of npdsch-MultiTB-r16 and ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17.

This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ce-ModeB-r13. A UE supporting this feature shall also indicate the support of ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17.

Content: This field indicates whether the UE supports HARQ feedback transmission for the first SPS PDSCH transmission after activation when operating in coverage enhancement mode A. A UE supporting this feature shall also indicate the support of ce-ModeA-r13 and ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17.
Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > IoT NTN parameters > ntn-UplinkHarq-ModeB-r18
Content: This field indicates whether the UE supports HARQ Mode B. This field is only applicable if the UE supports ce-ModeA-r13 or any ue-Category-NB. For a UE indicating support of ce-ModeA-r13, this field also indicates whether the UE supports the corresponding LCP restrictions for uplink transmission. A UE supporting this feature shall also indicate the support of ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17.
Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > IoT NTN parameters > ntn-HarqEnhNGSO-Support-r18
Content: This field indicates whether the UL and DL HARQ process enhancements that are indicated as supported are applicable in NGSO scenarios for UE indicating support of GSO and NGSO scenarios. If this field is not included, the UL and DL HARQ process enhancements that are indicated as supported are not applicable in NGSO scenario. This field is only applicable if the UE supports at least one of ntn-RRC-HarqDisableSingleTB-r18, ntn-OverriddenHarqDisableSingleTB-r18, ntn-DCI-HarqDisableSingleTB-r18, ntn-RRC-HarqDisableMultiTB-r18, ntn-OverriddenHarqDisableMultiTB-r18, ntn-DCI-HarqDisableMultiTB-r18, ntn-RRC-HarqDisableSingleTB-CE-ModeA-r18, ntn-RRC-HarqDisableSingleTB-CE-ModeB-r18, ntn-OverriddenHarqDisableSingleTB-CE-ModeB-r18, ntn-DCI-HarqDisableSingleTB-CE-ModeB-r18, ntn-RRC-HarqDisableMultiTB-CE-ModeA-r18, ntn-RRC-HarqDisableMultiTB-CE-ModeB-r18, ntn-OverriddenHarqDisableMultiTB-CE-ModeB-r18, ntn-DCI-HarqDisableMultiTB-CE-ModeB-r18 and ntn-UplinkHarq-ModeB-r18.
Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > IoT NTN parameters > ntn-Triggered-GNSS-Fix-r18
Content: This field indicates whether the UE supports network triggered GNSS position fix in RRC_CONNECTED as specified in TS 36.331 . This field is only applicable if the UE supports ce-ModeA-r13 or any ue-Category-NB. A UE supporting this feature shall also indicate the support of ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17. If the UE indicates this capability, the UE shall support the following enhancements: UE reports GNSS position fix time duration for measurement in RRCConnectionSetupComplete (-NB), RRCConnectionResumeComplete (-NB), and RRCConnectionReestablishmentComplete (-NB) and RRCConnectionReconfigurationComplete messages; UE receives GNSS measurement trigger from eNB; UE re-acquires GNSS position fix within a configured gap; UE reports the remaining GNSS validity duration with MAC CE in RRC_CONNECTED.
Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > IoT NTN parameters > ntn-Autonomous-GNSS-Fix-r18
Content: This field indicates whether the UE supports autonomous GNSS position fix in RRC_CONNECTED as specified in TS 36.331 . This field is only applicable if the UE supports ce-ModeA-r13 or any ue-Category-NB. A UE supporting this feature shall also indicate the support of ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17. If the UE indicates this capability, the UE shall support the following enhancements: UE reports GNSS position fix time duration for measurement in RRCConnectionSetupComplete (-NB), RRCConnectionResumeComplete (-NB), and RRCConnectionReestablishmentComplete (-NB) and RRCConnectionReconfigurationComplete messages; UE re-acquires GNSS autonomously (when configured by the network) if it does not receive eNB GNSS measurement trigger; UE reports the remaining GNSS validity duration with MAC CE in RRC_CONNECTED.
Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > IoT NTN parameters > ntn-UplinkTxExtension-r18
Content: This field indicates whether the UE supports to perform UL transmission in a duration after original GNSS validity duration expires without GNSS re-acquisition as specified in TS 36.331 . This field is only applicable if the UE supports ce-ModeA-r13 or any ue-Category-NB. A UE supporting this feature shall also indicate the support of ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17.
Title Path: UE radio access capability parameters > Parameters independent of the field ue-Category and ue-CategoryDL / ue-CategoryUL > IoT NTN parameters > ntn-GNSS-EnhNGSO-Support-r18
Content: This field indicates whether the GNSS measurement enhancements in RRC_CONNECTED that are indicated as supported are applicable in NGSO scenario for UE indicating support of GSO and NGSO scenarios. If this field is not included, the GNSS measurement enhancements in RRC_CONNECTED that are indicated as supported are not applicable in NGSO scenario. This field is only applicable if the UE supports at least one of ntn-Triggered-GNSS-Fix-r18, ntn-Autonomous-GNSS-Fix-r18 and ntn-UplinkTxExtension-r18.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters
Content: The following clauses list the optional UE features not having UE radio access capability. NOTE: This clause does not yet contain complete analysis of all features of this release of specification.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > CSG features
Content: It is optional for UE to support some parts of CSG cell and hybrid cell reselection features as specified in TS 36.331 , clause B.2.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > PWS features > ETWS
Content: It is optional for UE to support ETWS reception as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > PWS features > CMAS
Content: It is optional for UE to support CMAS reception as specified in TS 36.331 . It is optional for a CMAS-capable UE to support Geofencing information (warningAreaCoordinates-r15) as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > PWS features > KPAS
Content: It is optional for UE to support KPAS reception as specified in TS 36.331 . The Korean Public Alert System (KPAS) uses the same AS mechanisms as defined for CMAS. Therefore a KPAS-capable UE shall support all behaviour that is included in TS 36.331 and TS 36.304 for a CMAS-capable UE.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > PWS features > EU-Alert
Content: It is optional for UE to support EU-Alert reception as specified in TS 36.331 . The Europearn Union Warning System EU-Alert uses the same AS mechanisms as defined for CMAS. Therefore a EU-Alert-capable UE shall support all behaviour that is included in TS 36.331 and TS 36.304 for a CMAS-capable UE.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > MBMS features
Content: It is optional for UE to support MBMS procedures as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > MBMS features > MBMS Service Continuity
Content: It is optional for UE to support MBMS Service Continuity for UEs supporting MBMS as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > MBMS features > MBMS reception with 256QAM
Content: It is optional to support MBMS reception with 256QAM for UEs supporting MBMS. A UE which supports MBMS reception with 256QAM shall also support dl-256QAM-r12 as specified in TS 36.331 , except UEs configured to operate in Receive Only Mode as defined in TS 23.246 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > MBMS features > PBCH repetition in CAS
Content: It is optional to support PBCH repetition in CAS for UEs supporting MBMS as specified in TS 36.211 . A UE which supports PBCH repetition in CAS shall also support fembmsDedicatedCell-r14 as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > MBMS features > PDCCH AL16 for CAS in MBMS-dedicated cell
Content: It is optional to support of PDCCH AL16 for CAS in MBMS-dedicated cell for UEs supporting MBMS as specified in TS 36.211 . A UE which supports PDCCH AL16 for CAS in MBMS-dedicated cell shall also support fembmsDedicatedCell-r14 as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > MBMS features > Semi-static CFI indication in MIB
Content: It is optional to support semi-static CFI indication in MIB for UEs supporting MBMS as specified in TS 36.331 . A UE which supports semi-static CFI indication in MIB shall also support fembmsDedicatedCell-r14 as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > MBMS features > MBMS reception using Receive Only Mode
Content: It is optional to support indication of MBMS reception using Receive Only Mode in an MBMSInterestIndication message for UEs supporting MBMS as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > UE receiver features > MMSE with IRC receiver
Content: It is optional for UE to support MMSE with IRC receiver for all PDSCH transmission modes except for transmission mode 9.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > UE receiver features > MMSE with IRC receiver for PDSCH transmission mode 9
Content: It is optional for UE to support MMSE with IRC receiver for PDSCH transmission mode 9, if the UE supports MMSE with IRC receiver as described in clause 6.6.1.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > UE receiver features > Single-user MIMO interference mitigation advanced receiver for UEs with 2 receiver antenna ports
Content: It is optional for UE with 2 receiver antenna ports to support receivers with enhanced inter-stream interference suppression for SU-MIMO PDSCH with rank 2 (Enhanced performance requirements Type C for 2 receiver antenna ports capable UEs in the TS 36.101 ).
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > UE receiver features > Single-user MIMO interference mitigation advanced receiver for UEs with 4 receiver antenna ports
Content: It is optional for UE with 4 receiver antenna ports to support R-ML receivers with enhanced inter-stream interference suppression for SU-MIMO PDSCH with rank 2, 3, and 4 (Enhanced performance requirements Type C for 4 receiver antenna ports capable UEs in the TS 36.101 ).
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > UE receiver features > MMSE-IRC DL Control Channel interference mitigation receiver for UEs with 4 receiver antenna ports
Content: It is optional for UE with 4 receiver antenna ports to support MMSE-IRC DL Control Channel interference mitigation receivers for UEs with 4 receiver ports (Enhanced downlink control channel performance requirements Type A for 4 receiver antenna ports capable UEs in the TS 36.101 ).
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > RRC Connection > RRC Connection Reject with deprioritisation
Content: It is optional for UE to support RRCConnectionReject with deprioritisationReq as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > RRC Connection > RRC Connection Establishment Failure Temporary Qoffset
Content: It is optional for UE to support RRC Connection Establishment failure temporary Qoffset as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > RRC Connection > mo-VoiceCall establishment cause for mobile originating MMTEL video
Content: It is optional for UE to support mo-VoiceCall establishment cause for mobile originating MMTEL video as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > RRC Connection > mo-VoiceCall establishment cause for mobile originating MMTEL voice
Content: It is optional for UE to support mo-VoiceCall establishment cause for mobile originating MMTEL voice as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > RRC Connection > RRC Connection Re-establishment for the Control Plane CIoT EPS Optimization
Content: It is optional for UE to support RRCConnectionReestablishment for the Control Plane CIoT EPS Optimization as specified in TS 36.331 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Other features > System Information Block Type 16
Content: It is optional for UE, including UEs of any ue- Category-NB, to support the reception of SystemInformationBlockType16 as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Other features > QCI1 indication in Radio Link Failure Report
Content: It is optional for the UE to include drb-EstablishedWithQCI-1 in RLF-Report as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Other features > Enhanced random access power control
Content: It is optional for UE to support enhanced random access power control for FDD as specified in TS 36.321 and TS 36.213 , clauses 16.2.1.1.1 and 16.3.1. This feature is only applicable if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Other features > MO-EDT for Control Plane CIoT EPS Optimization
Content: It is optional for UE to support MO-EDT for Control Plane CIoT EPS optimizations as specified in TS 24.301 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ce-ModeA-r13, or for FDD if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Other features > Enhanced PHR
Content: It is optional for UE to support enhanced PHR in MSG3 for FDD, as defined in TS 36.321 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Other features > Resynchronization Signals
Content: It is optional for UE to support resynchronization signals, as defined in TS 36.211 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ce-ModeA-r13.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Other features > Measurement gaps for higher UE velocity
Content: It is optional for UE to support measurement gaps for higher UE velocity, as defined in TS 36.331 and TS 36.133 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ce-ModeA-r13.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Other features > MT-EDT for Control Plane CIoT EPS Optimisation
Content: It is optional for UE to support MT-EDT for Control Plane CIoT EPS Optimisation, as defined in TS 24.301 . If the UE supports 'MT-EDT for Control Plane CIoT EPS Optimisation' it shall support 'MO-EDT for Control Plane CIoT EPS Optimisation' as described in clause 6.8.4. This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ce-ModeA-r13, or for FDD if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Other features > MT-EDT for User Plane CIoT EPS Optimisation
Content: It is optional for UE to support MT-EDT for User Plane CIoT EPS Optimisation, as defined in TS 24.301 . If the UE supports 'MT-EDT for User Plane CIoT EPS Optimisation' it shall support earlyData-UP-r15 as described in clause 4.3.8.7. This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ce-ModeA-r13, or for FDD if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Other features > Reduced MIB/SIB1-BR acquisition time
Content: It is optional for UE to support reduced MIB/SIB1-BR acquisition time requirements as specified in TS 36.133 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ce-ModeB-r13.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Other features > High speed dedicated network features
Content: It is optional for UE to support HSDN cell reselection handling in RRC_IDLE and RRC_INACTIVE (if the UE supports eutra-5GC-r15) as specified in TS 36.304 and TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Other features > Carrier specific NRSRP thresholds for NPRACH resource selection
Content: It is optional for UE to support carrier specific NRSRP thresholds for NPRACH resource selection as specified in TS 36.321 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB and multiCarrier-NPRACH-r14 or multiCarrierPagingTDD-r15.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Other features > Protection against improper reselection to GERAN/UTRAN
Content: It is optional for UE to support protection against improper reselection to GERAN/UTRAN as specified in TS 36.304 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > SON features > Radio Link Failure Report for inter-RAT MRO
Content: It is optional for UE to include previousUTRA-CellId and selectedUTRA-CellId in RLF-Report upon request from the network as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > SON features > Radio Link Failure Report for NB-IoT
Content: It is optional for UE to support the storage of RLF-Report and the reporting in UEInformationResponse message as specified in TS 36.331 when connected to EPC. This feature is only applicable if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > SON features > Radio Link Failure Report for inter-RAT MRO NR
Content: It is optional for UE to include previousNR-PCellId, failedNR-PCellId and nrReconnectCellId in RLF-Report upon request from the network as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > SON features > LTE RLF report for voice fallback in LTE
Content: It is optional for UE to include voiceFallbackHO in RLF-Report upon request from the network as specified in TS 36.331 , when an RLF occurs shortly after successful HO from NR to E-UTRAN for voice fallback.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Mobility state features > Mobility history information storage
Content: It is optional for UE to support the storage of mobility history information and the reporting in UEInformationResponse message as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Sidelink features > Sidelink Relay UE operation
Content: It is optional for UE to support sidelink relay UE operation as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Sidelink features > Sidelink Remote UE operation
Content: It is optional for UE to support sidelink remote UE operation as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Sidelink features > Sidelink discovery gap
Content: It is optional for UE to support sidelink discovery gaps as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Sidelink features > Enhanced sidelink resource selection
Content: It is optional for limited TX capability UE to support enhanced sidelink resource selection with carrier aggregation as specified in clause 5.14.1.1 of TS 36.321 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Sidelink features > Short-term time-scale TDM for in-device coexistence
Content: It is optional for UE to support prioritization between LTE sidelink transmission/reception and NR sidelink transmission/reception. This feature is only applicable if the UE supports at least one of sl-Reception-r16, sl-TransmissionMode1-r16 and sl-TransmissionMode2-r16 as specified in TS 38.331 , and if UE supports LTE V2X sidelink in the band combination.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > DRX features > Extended DRX in RRC_IDLE
Content: It is optional for UE to support extended DRX cycle values up to and beyond 10.24 seconds and paging in extended DRX in RRC_IDLE as specified in TS 36.331 and TS 36.304 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Load balancing features > Redistribution in RRC_IDLE
Content: It is optional for UE to support redistribution in RRC_IDLE as specified in TS 36.331 and TS 36.304 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > SC-PTM features > SC-PTM in Idle mode
Content: It is optional for UE to support the SC-PTM reception in RRC_IDLE as specified in TS 36.331 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports UE category M1 or UE category M2 or if the UE supports coverage enhancements (ce-ModeB-r13 and/or ce-ModeA-r13) or for FDD, if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > SC-PTM features > Multiple TB scheduling for SC-PTM in Idle mode for NB-IoT
Content: It is optional for UE to support multiple TB scheduling for multicast as specified in TS 36.331 when connected to EPC. This feature is only applicable for FDD if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > SC-PTM features > Multiple TB scheduling for SC-PTM in Idle mode for CE Mode A
Content: It is optional for UE to support multiple TB scheduling for multicast as specified in TS 36.331 when connected to EPC. This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ce-ModeA-r13.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > SC-PTM features > Multiple TB scheduling for SC-PTM in Idle mode for CE Mode B
Content: It is optional for UE to support multiple TB scheduling for multicast as specified in TS 36.331 when connected to EPC. This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ce-ModeB-r13.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Idle mode measurements > Relaxed monitoring
Content: It is optional for UE to support relaxed monitoring in RRC_IDLE as specified in TS 36.304 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB or if the UE supports UE category M1 or UE category M2 or if the UE supports coverage enhancements (ce-ModeB-r13 and/or ce-ModeA-r13).
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Idle mode measurements > DL channel quality reporting in Msg3 for the anchor carrier
Content: It is optional for UE to support DL channel quality reporting in Msg3 for the anchor carrier for FDD, as specified in TS 36.331 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Idle mode measurements > Serving cell idle mode measurements reporting
Content: It is optional for UE to include measResultServCell-r14 in RRCConnectionRestablishmentComplete-NB, RRCConnectionResumeComplete-NB and RRCConnectionSetupComplete-NB messages as specified in TS 36.331 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Idle mode measurements > NSSS-Based RRM measurements
Content: It is optional for UE to support NSSS-Based RRM measurements for FDD, as specified in TS 36.211 and TS 36.214 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Idle mode measurements > NPBCH-Based RRM measurements
Content: It is optional for UE to support NPBCH-Based RRM measurements for the serving cell for FDD, as specified in TS 36.214 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Idle mode measurements > RRM measurements on non-anchor paging carriers
Content: It is optional for UE to support idle mode RRM measurements on non-anchor paging carriers for FDD, as specified in TS 36.133 . A UE supporting RRM measurements on non-anchor paging carriers shall also support NRS presence on non-anchor paging carriers. This feature is only applicable if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Idle mode measurements > NRS presence on non-anchor paging carriers
Content: It is optional for UE to support NRS presence on non-anchor paging carriers for FDD as specified in TS 36.211 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Idle mode measurements > DL channel quality reporting in Msg3 for non-anchor carrier
Content: It is optional for UE to support DL channel quality reporting for a non-anchor carrier for FDD in Msg3 as specified in TS 36.331 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Idle mode measurements > Assistance information for inter-RAT cell selection to/from NB-IoT
Content: It is optional for UE to support assistance information for inter-RAT cell selection to/from NB-IoT as specified in TS 36.331 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Idle mode measurements > DL channel quality reporting in Msg3
Content: It is optional for UE to support DL channel quality reporting of the serving cell in Msg3, as specified in TS 36.321 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ce-ModeA-r13.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Idle mode measurements > Relaxed RRM measurements
Content: It is optional for UE to support relaxation of RRM measurements for serving cell while using WUS, as specified in TS 36.133 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ce-ModeA-r13 and (wakeUpSignal-r15 or groupWakeUpSignal-r16 or wakeUpSignal-TDD-r15 or groupWakeUpSignalTDD-r16).
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Idle mode measurements > RSS based measurement improvement
Content: It is optional for UE to support improved DL RSRP measurement accuracy through use of RSS in RRC_IDLE as specified in TS 36.133 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports resynchronization signals as defined in 6.8.8.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > Idle mode measurements > RSS based measurement in paging MPDCCH narrowband
Content: It is optional for UE to support measurement of the neighbour cell RSS in the same narrowband as the paging MPDCCH narrowband in RRC_IDLE as specified in TS 36.133 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports resynchronization signals as defined in 6.8.8.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > E-UTRA/5GC features > RRC Connection Re-establishment for the Control Plane CIoT 5GS Optimisation
Content: It is optional for UE to support RRCConnectionReestablishment for the Control Plane CIoT 5GS Optimisation as specified in TS 36.331 . A UE supporting RRCConnectionReestablishment for the Control Plane CIoT 5GS Optimisation shall also support NB-IoT/5GC. This feature is only applicable if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > E-UTRA/5GC features > NB-IoT/5GC
Content: It is optional for UE to support NB-IoT when connected to 5GC. This feature is only applicable if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > E-UTRA/5GC features > MO-EDT for Control Plane CIoT 5GS Optimisation
Content: It is optional for UE to support MO-EDT for Control Plane CIoT 5GS optimisations as specified in TS 24.501 . A UE supporting MO-EDT for the Control Plane CIoT 5GS Optimisation shall also support NB-IoT/5GC or indicate support of ce-EUTRA-5GC-r16. This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ce-ModeA-r13, or for FDD if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > E-UTRA/5GC features > AS RAI
Content: It is optional for UE to support AS Release Assistance Indication (AS RAI) in Downlink Channel Quality Report and AS RAI MAC Control Element as specified in TS 36.321 when connected to 5GC. A UE supporting AS RAI shall also support NB-IoT/5GC or indicate support of ce-EUTRA-5GC-r16. This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ce-ModeA-r13 or if the UE supports any ue-Category-NB.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > E-UTRA/5GC features > Minimization of service interruption
Content: It is optional for UE to support minimization of service interruption including reporting to NAS of disaster roaming information for available PLMNs and Access Barring check for Access Identity 3, as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > IoT NTN Features > Cell reselection measurements triggering based on service time
Content: It is optional for UE camped on NTN cell to support triggering of early cell reselection measurements based on the service time broadcasted by the cell as specified in TS 36.304 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > IoT NTN Features > Discontinuous coverage
Content: It is optional for a UE camped on NTN cell to support discontinuous coverage as specified in TS 36.304 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > IoT NTN Features > Early RLF triggering based on service time
Content: It is optional for UE in RRC_CONNECTED in an NTN cell to support triggering of RLF upon reaching the service time broadcasted for the serving cell as specified in TS 36.331 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > IoT NTN Features > Neighbour cell measurements based on service start time of the neighbour cell
Content: It is optional for UE camped on NTN cell to support NTN neighbour cell measurements based on the service start time of the neighbour cell broadcasted by the serving cell as specified in TS 36.304 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > IoT NTN Features > UE autonomous release based on service time
Content: It is optional for UE in RRC_CONNECTED in an NTN cell to go to RRC_IDLE after RLF is triggered if the UE determines by implementation there is not enough time to finish the procedure of reestablishment due to the discontinuous coverage as specified in TS 36.331 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > IoT NTN Features > Cell reselection measurements triggering based on location for fixed cell
Content: It is optional for UE camped on NTN earth fixed cell to support triggering of early cell reselection measurements based on the reference location broadcasted by the cell as specified in TS 36.304 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > IoT NTN Features > Cell reselection measurements triggering based on location for earth moving cell
Content: It is optional for UE camped on NTN earth moving cell to support triggering of early cell reselection measurements based on the reference location and associated reference time and ephemeris broadcasted by the cell as specified in TS 36.304 . This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17.
Title Path: Optional features without UE radio access capability parameters > IoT NTN Features > GNSS measurements during inactive time
Content: It is optional for UE in RRC_CONNECTED in an NTN cell to perform GNSS measurements during inactive time of a C-DRX cycle. This feature is only applicable if the UE supports ntn-Connectivity-EPC-r17.
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > Access control features > SSAC
Content: It is mandatory to support Service Specific Access Control subject to common and per PLMN access barring parameters as specified in TS 36.331 , clause 5.3.3.10 for UEs which are IMS voice capable in LTE.
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > Access control features > CSFB Access Barring Control
Content: It is mandatory to support CSFB Access Barring Control subject to common and per PLMN access barring parameters as specified in TS 36.331 , clause 5.3.3.2 for UEs which are supporting CSFB to UTRA or GERAN.
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > Access control features > Extended Access Barring
Content: It is mandatory to support Extended Access Barring check as specified in TS 36.331 , clause 5.3.3.12 for UEs which are supporting an access subject to Extended Access Barring.
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > Access control features > ACDC
Content: It is mandatory to support barring check for ACDC subject to common and per PLMN barring parameters for ACDC as specified in TS 36.331 , clause 5.3.3.13 for UEs which are supporting an access subject to ACDC.
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > Access control features > EAB per RSRP
Content: It is mandatory to support eab-PerRSRP as specified in clause 5.3.3.12 of TS 36.331 for BL UEs or UEs in coverage enhancement supporting Extended Access Barring.
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > Emergency call features > IMS emergency call
Content: It is mandatory to support IMS emergency call for UEs which are IMS voice capable in LTE.
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > MAC features > SR mask
Content: It is mandatory to support configuration indicated by logicalChannelSR-Mask for UE which have set bit number 29 of featureGroupIndicators to "1" as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > MAC features > Power Management Indicator in PHR
Content: Power management indicator in PHR is mandatory to support for UE applying additional power backoff due to power management (as allowed by P-MPR see TS 36.101 ).
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > Inter-RAT Mobility features > High Priority CSFB redirection
Content: It is mandatory to support the RRCConnectionRelease indicating 'cs-FallbackHighPriority' for UEs which are supporting CSFB to UTRA as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > Inter-RAT Mobility features > GERAN A/Gb mode to E-UTRAN Inter RAT handover (PS Handover)
Content: It is mandatory to support at least parameter values corresponding to ue-Category 1 for UEs which are supporting GERAN A/Gb mode to E-UTRAN Inter RAT handover (PS Handover) as specified in TS 23.401 .
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > Inter-RAT Mobility features > SRVCC to E-UTRAN from GERAN
Content: It is mandatory to support at least parameter values corresponding to ue-Category 1, and ROHC profiles for an 'IMS capable UE supporting voice' as specified in clause 4.3.1.1, for UEs which are supporting SRVCC to E-UTRAN from GERAN as specified in TS 23.216 . NOTE: Requirements on functionality covered by Feature Group Indicators are specified in TS 36.331 , clause B.1.
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > Delay Tolerant Access Features > extendedWaitTime
Content: It is mandatory to support the RRCConnectionRelease with extendedWaitTime and RRCConnectionReject with extendedWaitTime for UEs which support Delay Tolerant Access as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > Physical layer features > Different UL/ DL configuration for TDD inter-band carrier aggregation
Content: It is mandatory to support different UL/ DL configuration for UEs supporting inter-band TDD carrier aggregation band combinations and for UEs supporting inter-band TDD dual connectivity band combinations within cell group(s) including at least two TDD bands.
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > Physical layer features > Full duplex for TDD and FDD carrier aggregation
Content: UE of this version of the specification shall be able to support simultaneous reception and transmission on different bands for each band combination including at least one FDD band and at least one TDD band.
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > Physical layer features > Simultaneous transmission of PUCCH and PUSCH across PUCCH groups
Content: It is mandatory to support simultaneous transmission of PUCCH and PUSCH across PUCCH groups if the UE indicates support for pucch-SCell.
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > Physical layer features > Simultaneous transmission of PUCCH in licensed spectrum and PUSCH in LAA SCells
Content: It is mandatory to support simultaneous transmission of PUCCH in licensed spectrum and PUSCH in LAA SCells if the UE supports uplink LAA operation. If the UE supports dual connectivity, this is applicable within each cell group.
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > Positioning features > OTDOA Inter-frequency RSTD measurement indication
Content: It is mandatory to support delivery of InterFreqRSTDMeasurementIndication as specified in TS 36.331 , clause 5.5.7 for UEs indicating support for inter-frequency RSTD measurements for OTDOA as specified in TS 36.355 and requiring measurement gaps for performing these measurements.
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > Other features > Logged MDT measurement suspension due to IDC interference
Content: It is mandatory to support Logged MDT measurement suspension due to IDC interference for UEs which are supporting logged measurements in RRC_IDLE upon request from the network and in-device coexistence indication as well as autonomous denial functionality as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > Other features > Support of extended reporting of WLAN measurements
Content: It is mandatory to support reporting of extended number of measurements of WLAN IDs for UEs which are supporting WLAN measurements as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > Other features > wlan-ReportAnyWLAN-r14
Content: Indicates whether UE supports reporting of measurements of unknown WLAN as specified in TS 36.331 . It is mandatory to support reporting of measurements of unknown WLAN ID for UEs which are supporting WLAN measurements as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > Other features > wlan-PeriodicMeas-r14
Content: This parameter indicates whether the UE supports periodic reporting of WLAN measurements. It is mandatory to support periodic reporting of WLAN measurements for UEs which are supporting WLAN measurements as specified in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > Other features > TA Reporting during Initial Access for NTN
Content: It is mandatory to support TA report during initial access for UEs which support ntn-TA-Report-r17 as specified in TS 36.321 .
Title Path: Conditionally Mandatory features > E-UTRA/5GC Parameters > Downlink SDAP header
Content: It is mandatory to support downlink SDAP header for UEs which are either NAS reflective QoS or AS reflective QoS (i.e., reflectiveQoS-r15) capable in LTE.
